## Face-detection debug console — OMRON OKAO Vision (`face_dbg_*`, not reachable non-invasively)
Maintenance/console command set for the body's face-detection engine. Identified by its rodata string pool at `0x7df443xx`–`0x7df451xx`: command names `face_dbg_start/stop/get/keep/reset/abort/prog/get_eye/dump/setting/log/pt/load/set_pos/set_info/get_pos/mdm/tune_lost/tune_get`, field labels (`confidence`/`angle`/`pose`/`eye_x`/`eye_y`/`detect_w`/`scale`/`aspect`), and the OMRON **OKAO Vision** SDK markers `face_dbg_okao_pt_version` / `face_dbg_okao_ss_version` (`"Version :"`/`"Major :"`/`"Minor :"`). Mode tokens: `type => FACE_STILL`/`FACE_NEWEST`, `TRACKING/TOUCH POS`, `EYE-AF ON`. Shares the rodata pool with sibling recorder modules (`rec_ps.c`, `rec_raw_*`, `rec_sat_*`, `REC WAVE *`).
| address | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7de4afcc` | `face_dbg_dispatch` | name-keyed command dispatcher for the face console. Saves caller `d0`→`d3`, `a0`→`a3` (a3 = command descriptor, name at `*(a3+4)`); logs the `"FACE"` banner via `face_dbg_trace_label 0x7de4afb8`; scans the 24-entry RAM registry `0x6044CA38` (`{handler@+0, char* keyname@+4}`, stride 8), `strcmp 0x7da836f1`(`*(a3+4)`, `*(entry+4)`); on match `calls *(entry)` with `a0=a3, d0=d3`. Same shape as `shell_dispatch 0x7da84eb8` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7de4afc2` | `face_dbg_put_hex` | **223 xrefs** — module-local hex-trace thunk used by nearly every `face_dbg_*` handler; 2 insns: tail-call `dbg_print_hex_routed 0x7d9999f9` then `ret`. Nearby label strings `"LOG RESULT"`/`"LOG HISTORY"`/`"0x"` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7de4afb8` | `face_dbg_trace_label` | thunk → `dbg_trace_line 0x7da886b4` (gated console emit); used to print the `"FACE"` section banner and per-command `"..._dbg_* in"`/`"out"` labels | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x6044CA38` | `face_dbg_cmd_table` (RAM) | 24-entry command registry scanned by `face_dbg_dispatch`; 8-byte entries `{handler, char* keyname}`, populated at runtime | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x6044CAF8` | `face_dbg_log_seq` (RAM) | u16 log-sequence counter: `face_dbg_dispatch`-cluster increments it (`inc`; `div 32` → modulo ring index at `0x7de4b040`) and stores back | `symbol-map.md` |

## Diagnostics — in-memory trace capture buffer
`0x7ddca749` (1047 xrefs) is a RAM trace-capture sink. When enabled it appends log strings to a
512 KB buffer, so the buffer is a **standing dump target** (its text accumulates in RAM).
| address / global | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7ddca749` | `trace_capture_append` | if enable `0x60711de8==1` and not-full `0x60711de9==0`: `strlen`(`0x7da8376d`)+`memcpy`(`0x7da837d6`) the string `a0` into buffer at base`+offset`, advance offset. Else fall back to `trace_log 0x7d996ee2` + `cap_test(0x10000,grp1)` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7ddca7f0` | `trace_cap_puts_hex32` | formatted capture print (877 xrefs): `trace_capture_append(prefix a0)`, then append `" "+u32_to_hex8(d2)+"\n"`. Sibling `0x7ddca819` formats a 16-bit value via `0x7da894f1` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7dd438e1` | `log_val_fmt` | value-logging wrapper (869 xrefs): logs message (`a0`, default ptr `0x7ded4e40` when null) + hex value (`d0`); routes to `dbg_print_hex_routed 0x7d9999f9` (console) vs plain `dbg_print_hex 0x7d999a53` by `console_present? 0x7dd53bb2` or flag `0x60709d54==1` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7dd53bb2` | `console_present?` | thin wrapper → mask-ROM `0x47001b0a`, returns a byte (debug/console-attached query); gates log routing | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x60711de8` | `trace_cap_enable` | byte, `==1` → record into the buffer | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x60711de9` | `trace_cap_full` | byte, set to `1` when offset would reach cap (locks further writes) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x60711dfc` | `trace_cap_offset` | u32 current write offset into the buffer | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x60711e00` | `trace_cap_base` | base of the ~512 KB capture buffer (cap `0x7FFFF`) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da836f1` | `strcmp` | unbounded byte compare `a0` vs `a1` (`movbu`+`setlb`/`leq` loop), returns signed `*a0−*a1` at first mismatch, 0 if equal. **1437 xrefs** — the app-cluster (`0x7da83xxx`) copy, sibling of `strcpy 0x7da83710` and `strlen 0x7da8376d`; distinct from core `strcmp 0x7d997773`. (Was referenced inline by the DBGLVL row; now pinned by disasm) |
| `0x7da83710` | `strcpy` | copy `a1`→`a0` byte loop until NUL (`setlb`/`lne`), returns original `a0`; app-cluster sibling of `strcmp 0x7da836f1` |
| `0x7da83636` | `memcmp` | compare `d0` bytes of `a0` vs `a1`: `a2=a0+d0` end ptr, byte loop (`setlb`/`lcs`), returns 0 if equal or `d0==0`, else signed `*a0−*a1` at first mismatch. **221 xrefs** — app-cluster (`0x7da83xxx`) mem primitive. Adjacent siblings: `memcpy 0x7da83654` (fwd copy `a1`→`a0`, `d0` bytes, ret dst) and `memmove 0x7da83669` (overlap-safe: picks fwd/back by `cmp a1,a2`) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7dba8da6` | 213 | `extract_bits` | **bitfield extract** `extract_bits(value=d0, lo=a0, hi=d1)` → `(value >> lo) & ((1<<(hi-lo+1))-1)`, i.e. bits `[lo..hi]` of `value` right-justified. Builds the `(hi-lo+1)`-wide mask in a shift-add loop, aligns it to `lo`, ANDs, then `asr lo`. **213 xrefs** — e.g. `(a0=4,d1=7)` = high nibble, `(a0=0,d1=3)` = low nibble of a packed status/config byte | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da836ab` | `strcat` | scan `a0` to its NUL, then copy `a1`→end-of-`a0` until NUL; returns original `a0`. App-cluster string primitive (completes strcmp/strcpy/strlen/strcat quartet) |
| `0x7e270db5` | `wformat_core` | **wide-char (UTF-16) `vsprintf` engine** (374 xrefs): scans the format over 2-byte units (`movhu`, step +2) for `%`(37)/NUL, emits literal runs and `%`-conversions into an output builder, accumulating the total wchar count (returned via the `a0` count-ptr). Uses `wcslen 0x7e22b159` + `wcscpy 0x7e22b131`. Backs the camera's wide-text paths (menus/filenames) |
| `0x7e22b159` | `wcslen` | count 2-byte units until the 16-bit NUL, returns the wchar count (`d0`); null-safe |
| `0x7e22b131` | `wcscpy` | copy wide string `a1`→`a0` (2-byte `movhu` loop) until 16-bit NUL; null-guarded |
| `0x7e22b0fa` | `wcscmp` | null-safe wide-string compare: `a0` null → −1, `a1` null → +1; else compares `movhu` halfwords until mismatch or NUL, returns sign of difference (0 = equal) |
| `0x7e266e2c` | `set_wstr_member_156` | **assign a pooled UTF-16 string to `obj+156`** (269 xrefs): arg `a1` = new wide string. Null → free-and-clear (`+156=0`, return 0). Else if `+156` already holds a string equal to the new one (`wcscmp 0x7e22b0fa`) → no-op return. Otherwise free the old buffer (`wstr_free 0x7e23c5d8`), `wcslen 0x7e22b159` the new, allocate `(len+1)*2` bytes (`wstr_alloc 0x7e23c5cc`), `memset 0x4700110c` it to 0, and copy the new string in. The pooled wide-string allocator is keyed by the owner's byte tags `obj+28`/`obj+29` (pool/class id passed to `0x7e22a2a3`/`0x7e22a369`). Generic "set wide-string property" method shared by many record classes | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da83656` | `memcpy` | app-cluster byte `memcpy(dst=a0, src=a1, len=d0)`: forward `movbu` copy until `dst==dst+len`, no NUL stop; returns. 363 xrefs. Distinct from mask-ROM `memcpy 0x47001000` |
| `0x7da8369a` | `memset` | app-cluster byte `memset(dst=a0, val=d0, len=d1)`: writes `d0` from `dst` to `dst+len` (`movbu`/`inc` loop), returns `dst`. 270 xrefs. Distinct from mask-ROM `memset 0x4700110c`. Immediate neighbors in this libc cluster: `0x7da836ab` = `strcat` (seek NUL of `a0`, append `a1`), `0x7da836d1` = `strchr`-style scan | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da82f64` | `atoi` | base-10 convenience wrapper over `strtol`: `atoi(str=a0)` → calls `strtol 0x7da82f75` with base `10` and a local throwaway endptr (null-equivalent). 271 xrefs — the common integer-parse entry | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da82f75` | `strtol` | **`strtol(str=a0, endptr, base=d0)`**: rejects base ∉ {0,2..36}; skips leading whitespace via the ctype table `0x7dbbdc40` (`btst 0x20` = isspace flag); consumes optional `'+'`(43)/`'-'`(45) sign; base-0 auto-detect (`'0'`→octal, `'0x'`→hex). Accumulates digits (ctype table also classifies digit/letter values) and returns the signed long (`d0`); writes the parse-end pointer through `endptr` when non-null. The ctype/character-class lookup table lives at `0x7dbbdc40` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da8935b` | 146 | `parse_hex_n` | **fixed-length hex-string → u32** parser (not NUL-terminated, unlike `strtol`). Args `a0`=char buffer, `d0`=max digit count (u16). Accumulates `val = val*16 + digit` (`asl 4`) over `[0,count)`, decoding `'0'–'9'`(48–57)→0–9, `'A'–'F'`(65–70)→10–15 (`+-55`), `'a'–'f'`(97–102)→10–15 (`+-87`); **stops at the first non-hex char** (`<'0'` or `>'f'` breaks the loop). Returns value in `d0`. Used to parse hex fields from fixed-width records/strings. Sibling in the same `0x7da892xx`–`0x7da893xx` numeric-helper bank: a length-bounded **decimal** parse loop (`×10` accumulate) and a `divu`-based u32→decimal-string formatter (`0x7da8932e`, 132 xrefs). 146 xrefs | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da83669` | `memmove` | overlap-safe copy (same args): forward copy when `dst<=src`, else backward copy (`add len` to both ptrs, decrement). 0 direct `call` xrefs (reached via pointer/tail) — sibling of `memcpy 0x7da83656` |
| `0x7da837d6` | `strncpy` | **CORRECTED** (rows above call it "memcpy"): copies `a1`→`a0` up to `d0` bytes, stopping early at the source NUL, then zero-pads the remainder of the `d0`-byte field. Used by `rtos_svc_err_log` / `trace_capture_append` to copy a bounded name |
| `0x7da8376d` | `strlen` | scan-to-NUL, returns `a1−a0` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7d999a53` | `dbg_print_hex` | gated by `0x6044a15c` (output enable, halfword): emit `" " + u32_to_hex8(d0) + "\n"` via `emit 0x7d996ee2`; alt path when `0x6044a15a==1` (1043 xrefs) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7d999aad` | `u32_to_hex8` | write 8 uppercase hex chars of `d0` to buffer `a0` (nibble loop, `+'0'`/`+'A'-10`) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7de2d376` | 336 | `trace_hex` | thin wrapper → `dbg_print_hex 0x7d999a53`(label `a0`, value `d0`) via marshalling thunk `0x7de2d173` (`a0=*rec; d0=*(rec+4); call 0x7d999a53`). Builds the 2-word record on-stack from the caller's `a0`/`d0`; the preceding read of BSS pair `0x7DF37940` (currently zero) is dead — its stores are immediately overwritten by the args (compiler artifact, not a real default). Sibling **`0x7de2d394`** is the string-only variant → thunk `0x7de2d187` → `emit 0x7d996ee2` (reads default pair `0x7DF37948`). 336 xrefs — inline "trace label + hex value" | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7de2d3b0` | 249 | `dbg_trace_gated` | **gated debug-trace front-end** (same family as `trace_hex 0x7de2d376`). Args: `d0`=category/channel, `d1`=u16 force/level (must be `0`), `a1`=filter selector (`1..6`, `0`=skip). Gate, all required: global trace-enable `(*0x6073cede)&1 == 1`, category `d0 ≤ max (*0x6073ced8)`, selector `≠0`. If gated through, tests the category against the current filter-mask byte `(*0x6073cee9)` via predicate `trace_filter_match 0x7de2fbef` (selector 1→bit0, 2→bit1, 3→bit2, 4→bit3, … ; `0xFF` always-on); on match emits through sibling `0x7de2d35a` (marshals default pair `0x7DF37938` → thunk `0x7de2d163` → `dbg_print` family). Sibling `0x7de2d3ee` = identical gate with reordered args. Reveals the **trace control block at `0x6073cedx`**: `+0e` enable bit, `+08` max category (u32), `+e9` filter mask (byte). 249 inline call sites | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7de2fbef` | — | `trace_filter_match` | trace-filter predicate for `dbg_trace_gated`: given mask byte `d0` and selector `d1` (`1..6`), returns 1 in `d0` iff the selector's bit is set in the mask (`d1=1`→`&1`, `2`→`&2`, `3`→`&4`, `4`→`&8`, …); mask `0xFF` short-circuits to always-match. Pure predicate | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7d996ee2` | `emit` / `trace_puts` | raw string output sink (1466 xrefs); takes string in `a0`. Emits byte-by-byte via `console_putc 0x7d9c51fc`, translating `\n`(10)→`\r\n` (CRLF) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7dd56a44` | 58 | `play_state_get` | **PLAY/MovPlay state-flag array getter** (module `0x7dd5`/`0x7dd6`, same subsystem as [`play_trace 0x7dd59aca`](#)). `play_state_get(d0=idx) → *(u32*)(0x6070F234 + (u8)idx*4)` — reads a u32 state flag from the SRAM array based at `0x6070F234`, indexed by zero-extended byte (`extbu; asl2`). Array sits immediately below the PLAY debug verbosity byte `0x6070F23C` (adjacent globals: ptr slot `0x6070F230`, flags `+4`/`+8`). Entries `[0]`/`[1]` seeded to `1` by helper `0x7dd56a16` (`mov 1,d0; d0→(0x6070F234); d0→(0x6070F238)`). **Sibling `0x7dd56a51`** = address-of form `return (u32)(0x6070F234 + (u8)idx*4)` (for RMW). Callers pass a literal index then test the result as a boolean (e.g. `0x7dd572a7`: `clr d0; call; cmp 0,d0`). 58 callers: **45×`0x7dd6` (MovPlay)**, 13×`0x7dd5` (PLAY). **Dead end:** per-index flag meanings not pinned — stringless numeric array | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7dd59aca` | 375 | `play_trace` | **PLAY-subsystem** level-gated debug trace-line: emits the message string `a0` via `dbg_trace_line 0x7da886b4` only when the PLAY verbosity byte `*(0x6070f23c) >= d0` (caller's message level). Tag strings seen at call sites: `"play_init in/out"`, `"PLAY: receive Load request"` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7dd59ade` | 366 | `play_trace_hex` | PLAY-subsystem level-gated hex logger: when `*(0x6070f23c) >= d0`, prints value `d1` via `dbg_print_hex_routed 0x7d9999f9`. Twin of `play_trace 0x7dd59aca` (msg vs msg+hex). `0x6070f23c` = **PLAY debug verbosity level** (adjacent `0x6070f23d` secondary); both cleared to 0 by the PLAY init/reset path at `0x7dd597f5` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7dd622d2` | 87 | `play_img_get_address` | **PLAY image-buffer address fetch by code** — thin API thunk (named by its own entry trace literal `"play_img_get_address"` `0x7DEEF3A7`, logged at level 1 via `play_trace 0x7dd59aca`). Takes a 16-bit `{group,item}` code in `d0` (`exthu`), forwards it to the routed value getter **`val_get_by_code 0x7de54fd8`**, returns the fetched value (a playback image/DRAM buffer address). Head of an **adjacent PLAY image-dummy-id API bank** sharing the `play_trace`+`val_*` shape: `0x7dd622ee` = `play_img_get_dummy_id` (traces `"...in"`/`"...out"` `0x7DEEF3BC`/`0x7DEEF3D5`, worker `0x7de552cc`, returns `0xFFFF` on error), `0x7dd6232e` = `play_img_free_dummy_id` (`0x7DEEF3F0`, worker `0x7de5530c`). These wrap the camera's routed value get/set system for playback image-buffer management | `symbol-map.md` |

If suppress flag `*(0x6044a15a)!=1` → `console_out(a0)` + a second fixed string; else formatted path `0x7dbbdb29` with `"%s %s"` / `"%s %08X %s"` (label, hex, suffix). Twin `0x7da886fb` = same via `emit 0x7d996ee2` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7d9c51fc` | `console_putc` | terminal byte sink: char in `d0`. Guard/flush `0x7d9b1645`, then push char into a **767-entry RAM console ring at `0xa0bba15c`** (write index halfword at SFR `0x8ff00084`) and the hardware debug FIFO at the `0x8ff000xx` peripheral window (status `0x8ff0007e`/`007c`). Bottom of the entire log pipeline (`trace_log_printf`→`console_out`/`emit`→here) | `symbol-map.md` |

## Soft-float / FPU coprocessor intrinsics
Compiler-emitted FP helpers using MN10300 `udf04`/`udf05` user-defined (coprocessor/FPU) ops —
confirms the firmware does real float math via the coprocessor, not pure integer emulation.
| address | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7da801ef` | `fp_log` | **Double-precision natural logarithm** `log(x)` (**62 xrefs**), fdlibm `__ieee754_log` port. `(double d0:d1)`. Domain guards via double-classifier `0x7da83ceb` (zero/sign): `x==0` → errno cell `0xa09f0984=2`, return `0xFFEFFFFFFFFFFFFF` (−DBL_MAX ≈ −∞); `x<0` (`btst 128,(7,a2)` sign byte) → `0xa09f0984=1`, return `+0.0`. Else: mantissa split at **sqrt(2)=`0x3FF6A09E6686BACD`** (compare `0x7da83c7f`); if `≥sqrt(2)` divide by sqrt(2) and set flag `d2=1`, else divide by `1.0=0x3FF0000000000000`. frexp exponent extract `0x7da83d02`. Horner polynomial (coeff table `0x7dbcdc44..0x7dbcdc6c`, stride 8, descending) via FP mul `0x7da83f80` + add `0x7da83a5c`; FP mul/scale `0x7da83a50`. Final: `poly + k·ln2` with **ln2=`0x3FE62E42FEFA39EF`** (0.6931471805599453), plus half-ln2 `0x3FD62E42FEFA39EF` correction when sqrt(2) flag set. `0xa09f0984` = libm errno-style domain-error latch (2=range/zero, 1=negative), SRAM region beside math-reentrancy `0xa09f0978`. Softfloat/math cluster module 0x7da8 (`__divdf3`, `fp_classify_df 0x7da8411b`, `memmove_bytes 0x7da8366b`). 62 callers dominated by ADJ imaging-calibration (26×0x7e74, 20×0x7e73, 6×0x7e72, 2×0x7e75). **Dead end:** exact poly identity vs fdlibm coeffs not bit-matched; callees `0x7da83a50`/`0x7da83f80` mul-vs-div roles inferred from context, not fully decoded | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da8425a` | `fp_cmp_a` | `udf04 d1,d0`-based FP compare intrinsic; returns `2×result` in `d1` (heavily used — 1015+ xrefs) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da84248` | `fp_cmp_b` | `udf05 d1,d0` FP compare intrinsic (sibling predicate) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da8424f` | `fp_negate` | `xor 0x80000000,d0` (flip IEEE-754 sign bit), returns `2×` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da84261` | `fp_classify` | IEEE-754 single decompose: mantissa `&0x7fffff`, exponent `&0x7f800000`; detects inf/NaN (exp all-ones) and subnormal/zero | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da839fe` | `fp_to_int_sat_d` | **Double → integer convert with saturation** (**108 xrefs**) — the double-precision analog of single `fp_to_int_sat 0x7da841e1` (same `{0,−1}` saturation convention, distinct from the standard `__fixdfsi 0x7da83984` which saturates to INT_MAX). `(double d0:d1)`: classifies via `fp_classify_df 0x7da8411b` (class bits→`d3`); NaN (`btst 6`) → `0`; sign/inf (`btst 1`) → `−1`; hi-word `a3 < 0x3FF00000` (`|v|<1.0`) → `0`; `a3 > 0x41E00000` (`≥2^31`) → `−1` (saturate). In `[1.0, 2^31)`: assembles the 64-bit mantissa into 32 bits (`d1<<11 | d0>>21`) then right-shifts by `shift = ((0x41E00000 − bias_exp_hi) >> 20)`. Neighbor `0x7da83a50` = double negate (`xor 0x80000000` on hi-word). Exact rounding/signedness convention not pinned (no AM33-2 FPU spec) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da839ee` | `fp_to_uint16_d` | **Double → unsigned 16-bit convert** (**95 xrefs**). `(double d1:d0)`: `mov d1,a1; call fp_classify_df 0x7da8411b` (class→`d3`, hi-word→`a3`), `call` the shared signed-convert tail **`fp_dtoi_core 0x7da83994`**, then **`exthu d0`** (zero-extend bits[15:0]) → `(uint16_t)(int)d`. `ret [d3,a3],12`. Compiler-emitted narrowing cast for `unsigned short ← double` (wide use in param/scale math). Sibling entry **`0x7da839df`** = identical sequence with **no** `exthu` → plain saturating `int32` (classify+core, raw `d0`). Both share core **`fp_dtoi_core 0x7da83994`**: signed double→int32 with INT_MAX/MIN saturation — NaN→`0`; inf (`btst 1`)→`0x7FFFFFFF`, then `add a1,a1`/`bcc` picks sign (neg→`inc`→`0x80000000`); `\|v\|<1.0` (`a3<0x3FF00000`)→`0`; `v≥2^31` (`a3≥0x41E00000`)→saturate; else assemble mantissa (`d1<<10 \| d0>>22`), shift by `(0x41C00000−exp_hi)>>20`, apply sign via `asr 31`/`xor`/`sub` (two's-complement). Soft-float cluster with `fp_classify_df 0x7da8411b`, `__fixdfsi 0x7da83984`, `fp_to_int_sat_d 0x7da839fe`, `__truncdfsf2 0x7da838d4`. **Dead end:** rounding mode (trunc vs round) not pinned (no AM33-2 FPU spec) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da841e1` | `fp_to_int_sat` | **Single-float → integer convert with saturation** (`__fixsfsi`-class; **111 xrefs**). `(float d0)`: classifies the operand via `fp_classify 0x7da84261`; NaN (`btst 6,d3`) → `0`; sign/inf (`btst 1,d3`) → `-1`; operand `< 1.0f` (`0x3F800000`) → `0`; operand `≥ 2^31f` (`0x4F000000`) → `-1` (saturate). In range `[1.0, 2^31)`: reconstructs the integer by shifting the mantissa+hidden-bit right by `shift = ((0x4F000000 − bias_exp) >> 23)` ∈ `[1,31]` (i.e. `31 − ⌊log2 v⌋`). Entry `udf14 128,d0` + compare `0x7FFFFFFF` first screens the raw NaN pattern. Callers pass a float and treat the result as a small int (e.g. `cmp 2,d0` bucket test at `0x7da80fe9`). Part of the soft-float lib; exact rounding mode (trunc vs round) not pinned (no AM33-2 FPU spec) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da84235` | `fp_op_udf02` | `udf02 d1,d0` coprocessor FP intrinsic; returns `2×result` in `d1` (same ×2 convention as `fp_cmp_a`; 689 xrefs). Exact op not pinned — no AM33 FPU udf-opcode spec (dead end on precise semantics) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da8422e` | `fp_op_udf03` | `udf03 d1,d0` single-precision FPU binary op (283 xrefs); plain IEEE-754 result in `d0`, `2×` copy in `d1`. **Encoding insight**: this and its siblings `fp_op_udf02`/`fp_cmp_a`/`fp_cmp_b` are all one 4-byte FPU instruction `f6 X4 81 e5` (where `81 e5` = `mov d0,d1; add d1,d1`, the ×2 tail; objdump lacking AM33-2 FPU support mis-splits each into `udfNN d1,d0`+`mov`+`add`). The four differ only in byte-2 high nibble — `24`/`34`/`44`/`54` = four distinct single-float binary ops (the `__addsf3`/`__subsf3`/`__mulsf3`/`__divsf3` set). Exact nibble→op assignment still open (needs on-hardware test; no FPU opcode spec) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da84241` / `0x7da8423c` | `fp_cmp_udf06` | Single-precision FP **compare/test** predicate (`udf06 d0,d1`, hot entry `…4241` has **163 xrefs**). **Flag-setting, not value-returning** — unlike the `f6 X4 81 e5` ×2 arithmetic bank above, `udf06` is a bare 2-byte op (`f6 61`) with no `mov d0,d1;add d1,d1` tail; all 3 sampled callers (`0x7da80ac8/ad1/b47`) do `call …4241` then an immediate `bne` with no intervening `cmp`, so `udf06` itself sets the CPU condition flags from `d0`. Entry `…4241` seeds `d1=0` first = compare-with-zero / is-zero / operand-validity test (callers branch away on nonzero/unordered); sibling `…423c` = same op without the zero seed (compare `d0` vs caller's `d1`). Exact predicate (equal / ordered / NaN-check) not pinned — no AM33-2 FPU udf-opcode spec (dead end), but flag-set + `bne` usage confirms compare-class, not arithmetic | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da83a5c` | `__adddf2` | **double-precision** soft-float **add** (364 xrefs; emulated-double family, not the coprocessor path). Decomposes both IEEE-754 doubles (exp `lsr20 &2047`, mantissa `&0xFFFFF`+implicit `0x100000`), classifies each operand (zero/inf/nan → class bits 1/2/4), handles specials (NaN propagate, inf±inf, signed-zero), else aligns the mantissas by exponent difference and adds/subtracts, renormalizes and repacks the result double |
| `0x7da83a50` | `__subdf2` | **double-precision** soft-float **subtract** (356 xrefs): negates operand b (`xor 0x80000000` on its high word) then falls straight into `__adddf2 0x7da83a5c` — the standard `sub = a + (−b)` entry |
| `0x7da83d02` | `__divdf3` | **double-precision** soft-float **divide** (571 xrefs; same emulated-double family as `__muldf2`, not the coprocessor path). Unpacks both IEEE-754 doubles (exp `lsr20 &2047`, mantissa + implicit `0x100000`), classifies specials (zero/inf/nan → bits 1/2/4): NaN→hi `0x7ff80000`, inf/0 quotient → hi `0x7ff00000` or 0. Else **subtracts** exponents (`ea-eb`, range-checks `>1024`→inf, `<-1075`→0), normalizes both mantissas, and divides via repeated hardware `divu d3,…` (restoring long division of the 52-bit mantissas, remainder-corrected) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da83f80` | `__muldf2` | **double-precision** soft-float **multiply** (763 xrefs; pure integer, NOT the udf04/05 coprocessor path — doubles are emulated, singles use the coprocessor). Decodes two IEEE-754 doubles (11-bit exp `lsr20 &2047`, 52-bit mantissa), classifies operands (zero/inf/nan → bits 1/2/4), returns NaN hi `0x7ff80000` or Inf `0x7ff00000` for specials, else adds biased exps (overflow >3069→inf, underflow <970→0) and `mulu` the mantissas | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da84303` | 316 | `__floatsisf` | **int32 → float32** (single-precision) conversion, done in one **udf coprocessor** op (`udf14 64,d0`; `-m mn10300`/`-M am33-2` both decode it as `udf14`, i.e. an AM33 user-defined/DSP instruction — consistent with the "singles use the coprocessor" note on `__muldf2`). Leaf: `udf14 64,d0; retf`. Callers pass an integer and consume the result in float math. 316 xrefs | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da8430c` | — | `__floatunsihf?` | **u16 → float32**: `exthu d0` (zero-extend 16→32) then the same `udf14 64,d0` single-precision convert; `retf`. Unsigned-short variant of `__floatsisf` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da8434b` | 147 | `__floatunsisf` | **uint32 → float32** (single-precision). Branches on bit31 (`or d0,d0; bns`): **bit31 clear** (value < 2³¹, fits as signed-positive) → hardware fast path `udf14 64,d0` (same op as `__floatsisf 0x7da84303`); **bit31 set** (value ≥ 2³¹, signed convert would be wrong) → **software normalize** of the full 32-bit magnitude: seed exponent word `a0 = 0x4B000000` (2²³ scale), shift the integer into the 24-bit significand (right-shift+exp-bump while top byte `0xFF000000` set; left-shift by 16/8 with exp-decrement while small; final `btst 0x800000` loop lands the implicit bit), round-to-nearest-even (`add d1,d1` guard/round bits + `btst 1` tie, `inc d0`, mantissa carry re-normalizes exponent), mask mantissa `& 0x7FFFFF`, `or` exponent → float bits. No sign handling (unsigned). Soft-float cluster with `__floatsisf`/`__floatsidf 0x7da842ac`/`__truncdfsf2 0x7da838d4` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da842ac` | 315 | `__floatsidf` | **int32 → double** (emulated, integer path — same family as `__muldf2`, not the coprocessor). Captures sign (`d0 & 0x80000000`), takes abs (`asr31` + `xor`/`sub`), seeds exponent word `0x41D00000` and normalizes the mantissa into the 52-bit field (shift-left loop decrementing the exponent until the implicit `0x100000` bit lands), repacks hi:lo with the saved sign. Handles `0`→+0.0 fast. 315 xrefs | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da84309` | — | `__floatunsidf16?` | **u16 → double**: `exthu d0` then `bra 0x7da842ac` (`__floatsidf`) — a zero-extended short reaches the signed int→double path as a guaranteed-positive value | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da842a2` | 124 | `__floatshidf?` | **signed short (s16) → double**: `exth d0` (sign-extend 16→32) then `bra 0x7da842ac` (`__floatsidf`) — a sign-extended short reaches the signed int32→double core. Signed twin of the `exthu` entry `__floatunsidf16? 0x7da84309`. Adjacent 1-op sibling **`0x7da842a5` = s16 → float32** (`exth d0; udf14 64,d0; retf`, the single-precision counterpart using the coprocessor convert of `__floatsisf 0x7da84303`). 124 xrefs — `(double)(short)` casts in sensor/param math. Soft-float cluster (`__floatsidf 0x7da842ac`) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da84313` | — | `__floatunsidf` | **uint32 → double** (emulated): like `__floatsidf` but no sign handling and exponent seed `0x41E00000` (2^31 range); `0`→+0.0 fast path, normalize-and-repack otherwise | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e13e7dc` | 96 | `tp_flags_update` | **Status-flag set/clear + change-notify** on the **`tp_` playback-page / video-buffer manager** object (`a0`=manager, `d0`=request): flag word at **`obj+0x3928` (14632)**. `d0==0` → clear the whole word; **`d0` bit31 set** → `word |= (d0 & 0x7FFFFFFF)` (set the named bits, bit31 is the "set" selector); **bit31 clear** → `word &= ~d0` (clear the named bits). Then re-reads the word and calls change-handler **`0x7e13d0d7`**, returns the new word. **Anchors the `tp_` playback image-page manager** (module `0x7e13xxxx`), a **CmpPls** component of the already-mapped **PlayCommonComponent "Cmp" framework** (cf. `rbtree_insert_7636 0x7e196b09`, `cmp_node_create_insert 0x7e1968cc`). Rodata trace API (`0x7e4051xx`–`0x7e4056xx`): `tp_setVBuff`/`tp_getVBuff`/`tp_freeVideoBuffAll` (video-buffer pool), `tp_getPageNo`/`tp_initPageInfo` (image page/picture tracking), `tp_initMngInfo`, `tp_layerOffsetClear`, `tp_rcvDrawImageEndEvent`/`tp_rcvPlayPictureEndEvent` (draw/play completion events), tag `CmpPls(%5d)` — the **image playback/review draw pipeline**. The large `+0x3928` offset ⇒ a multi-KB manager struct. 96 xrefs. **Dead end:** which bits of the `0x3928` flag word mean what (draw-busy / buffer-ready / abort …) not pinned — bits are numeric, no per-bit string | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e12d71c` | 77 | `cmp_flags_update` | **Status-flag set/clear/reset + decode** on a **Cmp (PlayCommonComponent) component** object (`a0`=component, `a1`=**pointer** to the 32-bit mask word `m=*a1`): flag word at **`obj+0x2a5c` (10844)**. `m==0` → clear whole word to 0; **`m` bit31 set** → `word |= (m & 0x7FFFFFFF)` (set the named bits, bit31 = "set" selector); **bit31 clear** → `word &= ~m` (clear the named bits). Then re-points `a1=&word` and calls **bit-decoder `0x7e12b6aa`**, returns the new word. Decoder `0x7e12b6aa` fans the flag word into **boolean status slots** — tests bits `1,2,4,8,16,0x100,…` and writes `1`/`0` per bit onto its caller frame (a decode-to-struct expander). **Sibling of `tp_flags_update 0x7e13e7dc`** (same set/clear/reset+notify idiom, bit31 selector; that one at `obj+0x3928` with change-handler `0x7e13d0d7`, mask passed as `d0`). This one is in module `0x7e12xxxx`, the **`CmpPCom_PlayCommonComponent.cpp`** subsystem (cf. `rbtree_insert_7636 0x7e196b09`, `cmp_node_create_insert 0x7e1968cc`, `cmp_registry 0x60450fa8`); the 7636B (`0x1DD4`) Cmp node is bigger than `+0x2a5c`, so the flag word sits inside the component instance. Caller spread: 58 sites in `0x7e12`, 19 in `0x7e13` (77 xrefs). **Dead end:** per-bit meaning of the `0x2a5c` word not pinned — bits are numeric, no per-bit string; the decoder's output-slot semantics (which bool = which component state) likewise unnamed | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e1968cc` | 158 | `cmp_node_create_insert` | **7636B Cmp-node create + tree-link worker** (companion to `rbtree_insert_7636 0x7e196b09`; same **PlayCommonComponent** 7636-byte `0x1DD4` node). Allocs via `operator new 0x7e206a0f`(7636), zero-inits (ctor `0x7e196b69` = `memset`), **memcpy**s the value template from stack arg `(32,sp)` (RAM thunk `0x47001000`, 7636 B), then links the new node into the component tree by **action/side code `d2`**: `2`→`0x7e19c3b7`, `3`→`0x7e19dc5f`, `1`→`0x7e19dc5f`+finalize `0x7e19cd58`+trace `"SEND_AFTERACK_PLAY"` (`0x7E415AD6`, via `trace_log_printf 0x7dfc01bf`); default → no-op. Args: `a0`=parent/tree ctx, `d0/d1`=key/code passed through to the link helper. 158 call sites across the `0x7e1xxxx` Play framework | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e196b09` | 249 | `rbtree_insert_7636` | **find-or-create insert** into a balanced (red-black) keyed tree whose value nodes are **7636 bytes** (`0x1DD4`) each. Writes the candidate slot `(a2)` from stack arg `(28,sp)`, probes via `0x7e196aa3`; if the slot is still empty, writes the other candidate `(32,sp)` and inserts via `0x7e196b31`. Both insert paths: alloc 7636B (`operator new 0x7e206a0f`) → **zero-init** node ctor `0x7e196b69` (`memset`, RAM thunk `0x4700110c`) → **memcpy** the 7636B value from the template (RAM thunk `0x47001000`) → link + rebalance (`0x7e19dc5f` side=1 / `0x7e19c3b7` side=2). Node type dispatched through a 0..10 jump table `0x7E41F4F4` (`0x7e196b78`). Called as a method on the component-registry global `cmp_registry 0x60450fa8` with the key (component id) in `d0`; **249 call sites**, concentrated in the `0x7e12xxxx` subsystem. **Resolved:** the 7636B value node is a **"Cmp" component instance** of the **PlayCommonComponent framework** — rodata in-cluster: `"…CmpPCom_PlayCommonComponent.cpp play_service start"` (blob at `0x7E3FD519`), `"BootMount Start"`/`"CmpMediaMount Start"`/`"CmpMediaUnmount Start"` (`0x7E3FD3E4`). So this is component register/find-or-create keyed by component id | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x60450fa8` | — | `cmp_registry` *(RAM global)* | pointer to the **PlayCommonComponent ("Cmp") framework** registry/manager — root of the keyed tree of 7636-byte (`0x1DD4`) component instances. Read inline (never via a getter) across the `0x7e12xxxx` subsystem (`CmpPCom_PlayCommonComponent.cpp`); components are added/looked-up by id through `rbtree_insert_7636 0x7e196b09`. Lifecycle trace labels in rodata `0x7E3FD3E4`: `BootMount` / `CmpMediaMount` / `CmpMediaUnmount` / `CmpTS` (mount/unmount component events, `play_service`). Component on-stack frames appear as ~7796-byte locals (e.g. `(7796,sp)` at `0x7e132dea`) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da8415e` | `__extendsfdf2` | **float32 → double** widening conversion (259 xrefs). Classifies the single via `fp_classify 0x7da84261` (class bits in `d3`): NaN (bit4) → double NaN hi `0x7FF80000`; zero (bit2) → ±0.0; inf (bit1) → double inf hi `0x7FF00000`; else normal — rebias the exponent (single bias field `0x3F800000` → double `0x3FF00000`, `asr 3`) and shift the 23-bit mantissa into the double's 52-bit field, then OR the preserved sign bit (`0x80000000`) into the hi word. Returns the double in `d0:d1` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da838d4` | `__truncdfsf2` | **double → float32** narrowing conversion (149 xrefs) — inverse of `__extendsfdf2 0x7da8415e`. Classifies the double via `fp_classify_df 0x7da8411b` (class bits in `d3`): NaN (bit4) → single NaN `0x7FC00000`; zero (bit2) → ±0.0 (`clr d0`); inf (bit1) → single inf `0x7F800000`; else normal — rebias the exponent (double bias field `0x3FF00000` → single `0x3F800000`, `asl 3`), round and shift the 52-bit mantissa into the single's 23-bit field (`and 0x7FFFFF`), overflow/underflow thresholds `0x47E00000`/`0x38100000`/`0x38000000`/`0x36900000` (→ inf / denormal / ±0), then OR the preserved sign bit (`0x80000000`). Returns the float32 in `d0`. Soft-float cluster with `__floatsidf 0x7da842ac`, `__floatsisf 0x7da84303` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da8411b` | `fp_classify_df` | **double** unpack/classify helper — double analog of single `fp_classify 0x7da84261`. Masks the double fields (`and 0xFFFFF` mantissa-hi, `and 0x7FF00000` exponent, `or 0x100000` implicit bit), compares exponent to `0x7FF00000` for Inf/NaN, and sets class bits in `d3` (bit1 inf, bit2 zero, bit4 NaN). Consumed by `__truncdfsf2 0x7da838d4` and the double→int path `0x7da83984` (`__fixdfsi`, INT_MAX `0x7FFFFFFF` saturation) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da84446` | 126 | `__gtdf2` | **double soft-float comparison** (greater-than variant). Two doubles A=`d1:d0`, B=`a1:a0`. NaN-aware: tests each operand's exponent (`hi<<1 >= 0xFFE00000` i.e. exp all-ones) and mantissa; if **either is NaN → unordered**, returns 0 so the caller's `A>B` test is false (caller `0x7dd8c7a8`: `call; ble →0 else →1` = `A>B`). Both ordered → tail-jumps to the ordered-compare core **`__cmpdf2 0x7da83c7f`**. One of a **multi-entry compare family** at `0x7da844xx` (gt/ge/lt/le — siblings `0x7da84415`, `0x7da84477`, `0x7da84494`, each returning the sign constant for its predicate) sharing two cores: `__cmpdf2 0x7da83c7f` (full ordered double compare: unpacks A,B, strips sign `&0x7FFFFFFF`, special-cases ±0) and sign/zero-return helper `0x7da83ceb` (returns 0 for ±0, else `±2` from the sign bit). Soft-float cluster with `__adddf2 0x7da83a5c`, `__muldf2 0x7da83f80` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7da84415` | 95 | `__ltdf2` | **double soft-float comparison** (less-than / less-class variant — complement of `__gtdf2 0x7da84446`). Two doubles A=`d1:d0`, B=`a1:a0`. Identical NaN-check preamble to the gt entry (`d2 = hi<<1`; `cmp -2097152` = `0xFFE00000` exp-all-ones test; `asl 11,d2 | lo`, `bne` → NaN) applied to both operands. **Differs from `__gtdf2` only in the unordered sentinel**: on NaN does `clr d0; cmp -1,d0` (flags = `0 > -1`, the *greater/positive* branch) so the caller's `A<B` test resolves **false** when unordered — mirror of gt's `cmp 1,d0` (*less* branch). Both ordered → tail-jumps to the full ordered core **`__cmpdf2 0x7da83c7f`** (returns −1/0/+1); `retf [d2,d3],24`. The 415↔446 pair both use the full `0x7da83c7f` core (strict lt/gt); the other pair `0x7da84477`/`0x7da84494` instead jump to the lighter sign/zero helper `0x7da83ceb` with `retf [d2],4` (le/ge-class). **Dead end:** exact lt-vs-le split between the full-core and sign-helper pairs not individually pinned (callers branch on the condition flags, not the `d0` value, so the `−1`/`+1` sentinel is the only discriminator captured). Soft-float cluster with `fp_classify_df 0x7da8411b`, `__fixdfsi 0x7da83984`, `fp_to_int_sat_d 0x7da839fe`, `__adddf2 0x7da83a5c` | `symbol-map.md` |

## MovPlay (movie-playback) debug-print bank
A bank of 5 gated `printf`-style trace emitters for the **movie-playback** subsystem, all tagged
`[movp]` and gated by a single bitmask register. The flag names come from the inline debug-mode help
text (`"movp debug print tools … movplay debug MODE [OPTIONS]"`, rodata `0x7DEF3EEC+`). Caller passes
`(d0 = flag-bit mask, d1 = value, a0 = label string)`; the `%08x` prefix is a context id from
`0x7d996ba7`. Line is built with `sprintf 0x7da82515` into RAM buffer `movp_trace_buf 0xA0A69DE0`,
then emitted via `dbg_trace_line 0x7da886b4`. Mask is set by the debug-mode command parser
`0x7dd6d687` (and `0x7dd6e0ae`).

| address / global | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7dd6ee92` | `movp_trace_val` | **primary MovPlay trace** (208 xrefs): if `(*movp_debug_mask & d0)` set, print fmt `0x7DEF3EB5` `"%08x [movp]%s %d(0x%x)"` (ctx, label `a0`, value `d1`, value hex). Sibling `0x7dd6eed1` = same fmt, 2nd route | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7dd6ee20` / `0x7dd6ee5a` | `movp_trace_label` | label-only variant, fmt `0x7DEF3EA7` `"%08x [movp]%s"` (same gate/route pair) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7dd6ef13` | `movp_trace_err` | error variant, fmt `0x7DEF3ECC` `"%08x [movp]ERROR!!! %s %d(0x%x)"` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0xa0a69ddc` | `movp_debug_mask` | DEBUG_MOV_PLAY_* enable bitmask (debug-register region `0xa0a6xxxx`). Bits (from help text): 0 `FUNC`, 1 `CALLBACK`, 2 `MAIN`, 3 `IF`, 4 `IMDFUNC`, 5 `TIME`, 6 `MOV_HEADER`, 7 `RING_BUFFER`, 8 `AVCHD`, 9 `AVCHD_API_PARAM`, 11 `TASK`, 12 `AVCHD_BRIDGE`, 13 `AVCHD_API`, 14 `OS`, 15 `AVCHDI`, 16 `AVMID`. Set via parser `0x7dd6d687` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0xA0A69DE0` | `movp_trace_buf` | scratch line buffer for the MovPlay trace bank (sprintf target) | `symbol-map.md` |

## Audio codec register driver (`se2` sound chip — EQ / power / wave play-rec)
The **movie-sound / audio-codec** low-level driver. Register-name pool `0x7DEE0500+` names a `se2`
sound chip: a 5-band EQ register bank `se2_5beq_r{0..4}{g,p,a,b,c}` (22 names), power regs
`ldo_vsel=` / `bg_vsel=`, `[reg_reset_softstart]`, `[mute_all]`, and a full audio-command verb list
(`Power On/Off`, `Play/Rec Init/Start/Stop/End/Complete`, `Set Volume`, `Set Output/Input Device`,
`Noise reduction for lens`, `Get Wave Header`, `Check Wave Header`, `Level Monitor`, `Semaphore
Wait/Signal`, `audio_status`…). Register accesses are mirrored into a (addr,data) **journal** so the
debug shell can dump the chip's programmed state via header `"[reg]adr | dat"` (`0x7DEE0656`).

| address / global | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7dd4e968` | `sndreg_set` | audio-codec register setter (206 xrefs): `(a0=obj, d0=index)`. `d2=index&0xffff`; if `index>175` → emit trace error `0x0610C002` via `trace_log 0x7d997b14`, return 1 (out-of-range — 176 registers). Else if dbg-flag `0x200000` set (`0x7dd42b1e`) → `log_val_fmt 0x7dd438e1` msg `0x7DEE0656` `"[reg]adr | dat"`, value `(index<<16)|*(u16*)obj`. Packs `d2=((index&0x7fff)<<16)|(*(u16*)obj&0xffff)` and calls `reg_journal_append 0x7dd423b2`; returns 0 on success | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7dd423b2` | `reg_journal_append` | append `(addr,data)` to the register-access journal: read counter `*(u16*)0x607096e8`; if `>=256` return 1 (full); else write 6-byte record `{u16 addr @(a1), 4 data bytes from d1>>24/16/8/0}` at `0x607096ee + count*6`, `inc count` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x607096e8` | `reg_journal_count` | u16 journal entry counter (≤256) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x607096ee` | `reg_journal_buf` | journal record array, 256 × 6-byte `{u16 addr, u32 data}` (adjacent to counter) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7DEE0500` | `sndreg_name_pool` | `se2` register-name + audio-verb string pool (EQ `se2_5beq_*`, `ldo_vsel`/`bg_vsel`, `[mute_all]`, `[reg_reset_softstart]`, play/rec/device verbs) | `symbol-map.md` |

## Text / BiDi character classification (RTL menu i18n)
A bank of per-character classifier leaves feeding a dispatcher `bidi_classify 0x7E22A5C3`, which reads
a `u16` character (`movhu (a2),d0`) and branches by class. The script ranges — **U+0590–05FF (Hebrew)**
and **U+0600–06FF (Arabic)** — plus the bracket mirror-pair test identify this as **bidirectional
(RTL) text layout** support for the camera's localized menus/captions. The ASCII-printable path uses a
perfect-hash property table. The dispatcher is the base-class method of a text-layout object hierarchy
(derived vtable `0x7E46EEDC`, ctor `0x7e25d503`); the vtable[0] thunk `0x7e25d53c` re-exports it, which
is why that 2-instruction thunk shows 209 xrefs.

| address / global | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7E22A5C3` | `bidi_classify` | character-class dispatcher: `movhu (a2),d0` → branch by script/type (calls the leaves below); base virtual of the text-layout class | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e22b601` | `get_bidi_classify` | trivial getter `return 0x7E22A5C3` (adjacent to `prop_code_static_lookup 0x7e22b60f`) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e25d53c` | `vt_get_bidi_classify` | vtable[0] thunk (209 xrefs): calls base ctor `0x7e2304ef`, then `return 0x7E22A5C3`; shared across derived text-layout vtables | `symbol-map.md` |

## VG (vector-graphics) client — UI draw-command engine
The **vector-graphics client** (`[VG CLIENT]`), an OpenVG-style path/fill draw layer that feeds a
graphics-accelerator HW interface (`vgcHwifConv_*`). It builds draw commands into per-primitive
command buffers, then flushes them to the HW converter. Module `0x7e86xxxx`–`0x7e87xxxx`; string pool
`0x7E8A7C00+` (`[VG CLIENT] !!! ERROR !!! => %s`, `cannot assign HEAP memory of the VG client`,
`image address is not 128 byte aligned`, `vgcHwifConv_incObjectRefcount`, `vgcHwifConv_moveCB`). This
is the low-level renderer behind the camera UI/menus (pairs with the BiDi text classifier above).

| address / global | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7e87c5ca` | `vgc_draw_path_fill` | **core UI draw primitive** (209 xrefs): appends a path command (type 34) then a fill command (type 36) to the VG command buffer. Flushes first (`vgc_cmdbuf_flush`) when state `a3 ∈ {1,2,3}` and ctx `+116 != 0` (sets busy flag `+77=1`). Each append goes through `vgc_cmdbuf_reserve`; on overflow (ret 2) reports `"vpath(%d)…over flow"` (`0x7E8A7D3D`) / `"vfill(%d)…over flow"` (`0x7E8A7D61`) via the client error-callback `*(0x60482468)` + `vgc_error` (code `0x1002`) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e86bce9` | `vgc_cmdbuf_reserve` | reserve/bounds-check a slot in a VG command sub-buffer: selects buffer by `d0` (`this+4` vs `this+40`), reads `cap+12 / ptr+16 / count+20`; returns 1=ok (→`vgc_cmd_write`), 2=overflow, 3=alt | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e867e46` | `vgc_cmd_write` | write the command payload into the reserved command-buffer slot | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e87b2aa` | `vgc_cmdbuf_flush` | flush/submit the accumulated command buffer to the HW converter (→`0x7e87511b`) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e8551f7` | 57 | `vgc_ring_set_head` | **VG command-ring write-position setter with bounds asserts** (same VG-client module + same primary ring descriptor as [`vgc_cmdbuf_put 0x7e8550c5`](#)). `vgc_ring_set_head(a0=ctx, a1=pos)`: `P = *(u32)(ctx+5836+16)` (the primary ring descriptor; `ctx+5836`=`0x16CC`), `pos→d3`. Two range checks, each followed by an indirect **assert-handler** call through fptr global `*0x60482408` (passes the check result in `d0`, `1`=in-range): **(1)** `d0 = (pos <= *(P+4) + *(ctx+5836+4)) ? 1 : 0` (pos within tail+length), **(2)** `d0 = (pos - *P <= *(P+12)) ? 1 : 0` (span from base within capacity). Then commits **`*P = pos`** (store new head/write-position). Ring fields match `vgc_cmdbuf_put`'s view (`P+0`=head, `P+4`=tail, length at `(4,ctx+5836)`). `*0x60482408` = shared VG assert/validation callback fptr (also used at `0x7e8540ac`/`0x7e854a14`/`0x7e85e4a7`…). All 57 callers in-module (**37×`0x7e85`, 20×`0x7e86`**); ctx is the `vgc_get_context 0x7e854da1` singleton. **Dead end:** semantics of the two bound fields (`P+4` tail vs `P+12` capacity) vs `vgc_cmdbuf_put`'s own field use not fully reconciled; stringless module | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e8550c5` | `vgc_cmdbuf_put` | **70 xrefs** — VG command-buffer **append-with-overflow-flush** (the low write path beneath `vgc_cmd_write`). Takes ctx `a0`; works two sub-rings — the primary descriptor `P = *(ctx+5836 +16)` (`ctx+5836` = `ctx+44+5792`) with head/tail/base fields at `P+0/P+4/P+24` and length at `(4,ctx+5836)`, plus the secondary sub-object at `ctx+44`. Computes the ring fill-level across those fields and compares to capacity `*(ctx+36)` (`cmp d0,d2; bcs`); when the pending command would overflow it takes the flush path: emit/copy the pending block via `0x7e8553e6` (128-byte frame) then submit via `0x7e855011` (ring free-space calc: head/tail at `P+0/P+20` + 32-byte margin; returns status, nonzero→error `jmp 0x7e8551d7`), sets overflow flags `*(ctx+5836 +36)=1` and byte `*(5813,ctx+44)=1`, resets ring fields (+20,+0), and on the hard-fail branch logs **`"##### command buffer overflow! #####\n"`** (`0x7e8a1774`, ref at `7e8551bf`). Self-contained in VG module (callers 37×`0x7e85`, 33×`0x7e86`). Pairs with `vgc_cmdbuf_reserve 0x7e86bce9` / `vgc_cmd_write 0x7e867e46` / `vgc_cmdbuf_flush 0x7e87b2aa`; ctx is the `vgc_get_context 0x7e854da1` singleton family. **Dead end:** exact semantics of individual ring fields (`P+0/+4/+20/+24`, `+32` margin) and the two overflow flags not separated | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e8639ea` | 65 | `vgc_err_latch_first` | **first-error-wins latch for the VG (vector-graphics) client** — leaf: `vgc_err_latch_first(a0=ctx, d0=code)`: `p = *(u32)(a0+24)`; `slot = *(u32)(p + 3776 /*0xEC0*/)`; **if slot==0, store `code`** (else leave the existing one). So the earliest error code sticks; later failures don't overwrite it. The 65 call sites all pass a constant status/error code in `d0` (e.g. `0x500 = 1280`), then latch it. All callers in-module (38×`0x7e85`, 27×`0x7e86`) — the same VG-client block as [`vgc_cmdbuf_put 0x7e8550c5`](#) / [`vgc_get_context 0x7e854da1`](#); the immediate neighbor `0x7e8639fb` builds a VG hardware command record (opcode words `0x80000000`/`0x000F0490`/`0x00FF0491`) and submits it through `vgc_cmdbuf_put`, confirming the module identity. Error sink field is `+0xEC0` of the sub-object at `ctx+24`. **Dead end:** the VG error-code vocabulary (`0x500`, …) and where `+0xEC0` is later read/reported not traced; stringless module | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e87ab61` | `vgc_error` | VG-client error reporter (`[VG CLIENT] !!! ERROR !!! => %s`); code in `d0` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e854da1` | `vgc_get_context` | **85 xrefs** — lazy-init accessor for the VG-client module **singleton context** at RAM `0x60481DC4`. Thin public thunk (`call 0x7e8518c6; ret`); **83 of 85 callers are in the VG-client block `0x7e860xx`/`0x7e861xx`** (same neighborhood/RAM region as the VG command-buffer cluster `vgc_draw_path_fill 0x7e87c5ca` … `vgc_error`, and the client-error-cb global `0x60482468`). Worker `0x7e8518c6`: if `*(0x60481DC4)==0`, build it — alloc via `0x7e88cdae`, then register **two 72-byte sub-objects** `0x60B05220`(idx1)/`0x60B05268`(idx2) through `vgc_ctx_add_slot 0x7e854b24` (each seeded `+4=48`, `+8=0x60B052B4`, `+20=idx`, body copied from **data template `0x7E858AA0`** — a byte blob, not a vtable), guarded by init-state word `0x60481DC0` (set to 5 when uninit); always returns the context ptr `0x60481DC4`. Slot accessor `0x7e851910` maps idx 1/2 → `0x60B05220 + 72*(idx-1)`. **Dead end:** exact VG-client class name (stringless module; identity inferred from caller locality + RAM-region adjacency to the labeled VG cluster, not from a literal) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x60482468` | `vgc_error_cb` | VG-client error/overflow callback object (vtable-dispatched: `calls *(0x60482468)`) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7E8A7C00` | `vgc_string_pool` | VG-client message/function-name string pool (`[VG CLIENT]`, `vpath/vfill`, `vgcHwifConv_*`, HEAP/alignment errors) | `symbol-map.md` |

## Exposure / AE (EXP) subsystem — debug flags & trace
The **auto-exposure / program-line (EXP)** subsystem's debug-trace control. A single flag word
`g_exp_debug_flags 0x607529a0` gates `[exp]`/`[EXP]` trace prints across the exposure/AE/AF pipeline;
code reads it through a getter and `btst`s individual bits. String pool `0x7DF65580+` carries the
APEX values and the `exp` debug-shell command tree.

| address / global | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7deca77e` | `exp_get_debug_flags` | getter `return *(0x607529a0)` (187 xrefs): callers `btst <bit>,d0; beq` to gate exposure trace prints (e.g. bit `0x800` → msg `0x7DF5E5C2`) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x607529a0` | `g_exp_debug_flags` | exposure/AE/AF **debug-trace + behavior flag word**; bit `0x08000000` = `ip_af_flag` print-request. Widely consulted (via the getter) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7deca787` | `exp_af_flag_poll` | if `g_exp_debug_flags & 0x08000000`: log `"[exp]ip_af_flag"` (`0x7DF65621`) then clear the bit (one-shot). Backs the `exp af <0\|1>` debug command | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7DF65580` | `exp_debug_strings` | EXP trace/command string pool: `[EXP] BV_18x18`, `bv_std`/`drange_offset`/`sdr_record`/`expand_bv`, APEX `bv/av/tv/sv`/`cv`, command tree `exp <P_Line\|CAM\|AE_TAG\|AF_AE>` with `table_id`/`capture_mode`/`tag_*`/`left/top/width/height`, `120fps AF ON/OFF`, `g_debug_af_flag` | `symbol-map.md` |

## Subsystem singletons (named via inline RTTI/trace strings)
Cells cluster in a **singleton-pointer bank** at RAM `~0x60454418`–`0x60454440` (and a nearby
`0x604543xx` group): each slot holds one lazily-constructed subsystem controller. Getters follow the
shape `a0=*(cell); if null → alloc N + ctor + store`. Classes that install a vtable can be named from
the inline trace string that follows the vtable (see CPlayZoom); vtable-less struct singletons cannot.
| address | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7e24831c` | `CPlayZoom_instance` | lazy singleton getter for the **CPlayZoom** playback-zoom controller (924 xrefs): cell `0x60454418`, 184-byte object, ctor `0x7e24825d` (base `0x7e2414c5`), vtable `0x7e4693a0`. Class named by inline trace string `"CPlayZoom::setDbZoomInfo_Lite Called."` right after the vtable. Followed by an accessor bank (getters/setters at obj `+12/+16/+20/+32/...`) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e24cc99` | `cplayzoom_set_f56` | **CPlayZoom field-`+56` setter** — `*(u32*)(instance+56) = d0`; getter sibling `0x7e24cc9f`. Part of the `0x7e24831c` CPlayZoom instance's accessor bank. **90 xrefs**, nearly all in play-mode navigate/select paths that run the idiom `playindex_set_grid8 0x7e2414d9` → `CPlayZoom_instance 0x7e24831c` → `cplayzoom_set_f56(0)` — i.e. the field is **cleared to 0** on navigation. Read sites (only 2: `0x7e3144dc`, `0x7e31671d`) test it as a boolean (`cmp 0 … beq`), so `+56` is a **state flag** (nonzero = some pending/active condition, cleared when the cursor moves). **Dead end:** exact flag meaning not pinned — no dedicated string; mechanism (set-0-on-move, boolean-gated read) is all that is certain | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e23dfec` | `singleton_604543e8` | lazy getter: cell `0x604543e8`, 336-byte **vtable-less** struct, ctor `0x7e23de5d` (defaults `+16=0xff`, rest 0). No class string (no vtable) → unnamed state/config struct | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e24ff2a` | `global_ptr_60454428_get` | plain getter `return *(0x60454428)` = **`g_gui_app`** (GUI-App singleton, see GUI App / WinMain section); paired setter `0x7e24ff33`, set once by WinMain init | `symbol-map.md` |

## MainSystem / RHP — shooting-session & interval (timelapse) state controller
String pool at `0x7E35D980`: `MainSystem`, `rhp_take_over : bootState %d` (`0x7e35d99d`), the
`hls %d %d …` format family + `Wait Function` (`0x7e35db90`), and the two state-clear traces
`clearIntervalShootingState state[0x%08X]` (`0x7e35dbb8`) / `clearPhotoSessionState state[0x%08X]`
(`0x7e35dbe1`). "RHP" takes over boot state; drives the photo-session and interval-shooting
(timelapse) state machines, each backed by a global bitmask word. The clear ops run under the
scheduler critical-section pair `lock 0x7dfc1a9e` / `unlock 0x7dfc1aac`, trace the state being
cleared via `trace_log_printf 0x7dfc01bf`, then AND-NOT the passed bits out of the global word.
| address | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7dfc12e4` | `clear_interval_shooting_state` | `lock 0x7dfc1a9e`; trace fmt `0x7e35dbb8` arg=bits(d2); `g_interval_state 0x6044cf70 &= ~bits`; `unlock 0x7dfc1aac`. Clears interval-shooting (timelapse) state bits | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7dfc1312` | `clear_photo_session_state` | same shape (sibling): trace fmt `0x7e35dbe1`; `g_photo_session_state 0x6044cf98 &= ~bits`. Clears photo-session state bits. Followed by a third clear variant at `0x7dfc1340` touching state word `0x6044cfac` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x6044cf70` | `g_interval_state` | interval-shooting (timelapse) state bitmask word | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x6044cf98` | `g_photo_session_state` | photo-session state bitmask word | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x6044cf34` | `g_mainsystem_observer_head` | MainSystem state-observer list head/singleton: broadcast loop (`cmp 0, a0` guard, ≤100 entries) notifies subscribers on state change; entries use shared observer-base tag `0x7e35d9c4` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7dfc1a9e` / `0x7dfc1aac` | `rhp_lock` / `rhp_unlock` | paired critical-section acquire/release wrapping the state-clear ops | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7E35D980` | `mainsystem_string_pool` | `MainSystem`, `rhp_take_over`, `hls %d…` fmts, `Wait Function`, clearState traces | `symbol-map.md` |

## GUI App / WinMain message loop
Top-level GUI application task. A large WinMain-style function (around `0x7e22b245`) allocates the
**2876-byte GUI-App object** (`alloc 0x7e22d42b`), stores it in the singleton bank
(`g_gui_app 0x60454428`, partner slot `0x60454240`), logs `[GUI App]:WinMain messageloop start`
(`0x7e45e16c`) via `0x7e334cab`, then pumps startup event ids 4352/4098/6400 through the GUI
event dispatcher. Hot virtual-dispatch shims forward into the app object's (large) vtable.
| address | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7e23075e` | `guiapp_vcall_440` | **207 xrefs** — virtual-dispatch shim: `calls vtable[440](a1)` on object `a0`, then halfword accessor `obj_geth_f4 0x7e22ff64`, `exth` (sign-extend) result. Hottest entry of the GUI-App dispatch bank | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e230771` | `guiapp_vcall_584` | sibling shim: reads `g_gui_app` via `global_ptr_60454428_get 0x7e24ff2a`, compares against obj field `+120`, then calls `vtable[584]`/`vtable[580]`. Null-guarded | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e224c04` | `guiapp_dispatch_msg` | **GUI message/event dispatcher**: `d0`=event id → `0x7e224ee0` (recv/post, -1 timeout), then routes by id (4098, 4352, …) updating GUI-DB flag bytes (e.g. `0x60772fcc`). Pumped by WinMain | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e22ff64` | `obj_geth_f4` | halfword field accessor `return (s16)*(a0+4)` (via `0x7e22fea6`); one of a thunk bank (`0x7e22ff6e`/`0x7e22ff7a`/`0x7e22ff87`… each `inc4 a0`→call) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e2cb6dc` | 125 | `evt_chain_dispatch` | **handler-chain event dispatcher** (`a0`=container, `d0`=event id, `d1`=arg). Walks the container's handler list starting at its iterator fields (`obj+28` {node,ptr} pair, begin/end compared two-word for termination); for each handler object calls its **`vtable[0]`** (`mov (a0),a1; mov (a1),a1; calls (a1)` with `a0`=container) and interprets the returned disposition code: **1** = pass / keep walking the chain; **2→return 1, 3→return 2, 4→return 3** = consumed, stop with that code; chain-end (iterator slot `==0`) stops. Return = final disposition `d2` (0 if nobody consumed). Event id `d0` (saved in `d3`) is special-cased against magic **`0x971D`** (an extra continue). Caller `0x7e2ca643` dispatches through the **singleton handler container `*(0x6045e258)`** (built/cached by `0x7e2cb535`). Lower-level observer-chain primitive under the GUI-App dispatch layer. 125 xrefs | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x60454428` | `g_gui_app` | GUI-App singleton instance (2876 B); getter `0x7e24ff2a`, setter `0x7e24ff33`; set once by WinMain init. Partner slot `0x60454240` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e45e16c` | `guiapp_string_pool` | `[GUI App]:WinMain messageloop start`, `[GUI]:waiting END REDRAW / not change GUIDB` | `symbol-map.md` |

## Sensor pre-processing / black-correction (BC) calibration
Imaging-pipeline subsystem for **optical-black / black-correction (BC)** and smear calibration.
String pool `0x7e78c580`: `SetPrePro` (set pre-processing), per-Bayer-channel sums
`CRSumear`/`CGrSumear`/`CGbSumear`/`CBSumear` (**smear** = sensor dark/OB artifact, R/Gr/Gb/B),
`%sCalib.prm` (calibration param file), `PRM_BC_DISABLE`, `BC_UNIT:OFF`, `bc exec 1/5/6`.
A bank of **14 thin command wrappers** each sets a command id and calls the packer `0x7e7241c1`,
which byte-serializes (cmd, args) into a stack message via `0x7e759138` then runs it through
executor `0x7e723ab9` (72-byte frame).
| address | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7e724377` | `bc_cmd_09` | **202 xrefs** — BC/pre-processing command #9: `exthu` arg → stack, cmd id 9, call packer `0x7e7241c1`. Hottest of the command API | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e72438e` | `bc_cmd_13` | sibling wrapper, command id 13 | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e7243a5` | `bc_cmd_12` | sibling wrapper, command id 12 | `symbol-map.md` |

## GUI panel manager — guiUtility::ShowPanelEx
Central GUI screen/panel switcher. `ShowPanelEx(newPanelNo, sub)` compares the request to the
current panel (`g_cur_panel_no 0x604601d0`, u16); if a change is needed it tears down the old
panel, posts a **panel-change event to "CA"** (camera-application) and records the new panel.
Named by trace `[GUI App]: guiUtility::ShowPanelEx  PanelNo : %03d >>>>> %03d` (`0x7e5dee86`);
failure path logs `[GuiTouch] guiUtility::ShowPanelEx changePanel Fail!!!` (`0x7e5deec4`).
| address | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7e337794` | `gui_show_panel_ex` | **199 xrefs** — `guiUtility::ShowPanelEx(d0=newPanelNo, d1=sub)`. No-ops when already on the panel (and flags clear); else tears down old panel via `0x7e33927d`, runs `0x7e3358a8`, posts the change event via `gui_post_panel_event 0x7e33581d`, stores `g_cur_panel_no=newPanelNo` / `0x6046031c=sub`, logs the `>>>>>` transition | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e33c7cf` | `gui_cur_param_get` | **70 xrefs** — leaf getter copying the GUI **current-parameter state** (3 fields of the global struct at `0x6084b4d8`) into caller out-struct `a1`: `+0`=u16 `*0x6084b4d8` (current id), `+2`=u16 `*0x6084b4da` (table-derived value), `+4`=u8 `*0x6084b4dc` (flag). Writer-pair setter `0x7e33c7b4` stores id(`d0`)→`0x6084b4d8` + flag(`d1`)→`0x6084b4dc`, then derives `0x6084b4da` via `0x7e33bbda`→`0x7e33bbab`, which indexes the **12-byte descriptor table `0x7e5f2810`** `{u32 id, u32 count, u32 ptr}` (ids 1/2/3/4/6/8… → sub-table ptrs in `0x7e5f1xxx`), with sentinel `d1==0x7fff`→40. Callers snapshot the state then act on field0 (e.g. `0x7e30b1f8`: reads back `(16,sp)` as an index). Spread 35×`0x7e30`, 18×`0x7e35`, 14×`0x7e31`, 3×`0x7e34` — GUI module cluster (`guiUtility`). **Dead end:** semantic name of the parameter/selection (id namespace 1–8) and of field `+2`/flag `+4` not pinned (stringless; `0x7e5f2810` sub-tables unlabeled) | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e33581d` | `gui_post_panel_event` | builds a **PostEventToCA** message: alloc (`0x7e3354f7`), `*(ev)=2` (event type), `+4=panelNo`, `+8=sub`, `+12=ctx` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e291175` | `gcpnl_get_comp` | **70 xrefs** (all in-module `0x7e29`) — bounds-checked **GCPnl component accessor**: `gcpnl_get_comp(panel=a0, idx=d0)` → `idx >= 73 ? NULL : *(u32*)(a0 + 792 + idx*4)`. Returns the child-component pointer at slot `idx` of the panel object's **73-entry component table at `+792` (0x318)**. Callers pass **constant slot ids** (e.g. 40, 41 at `0x7e2912a0`/`0x7e2912aa`), so slots are fixed widget roles. NULL : *(u32*)(a0 + 792 + idx*4)`. Returns slot `idx` of the panel object's 71-entry component table at `+792`; the following routine `0x7e276d57` is the same iterator body (`0x7e24ff2a`/`0x7e24ff41`/`0x7e22be56`) as the `0x7e29` twin. Module `0x7e27` = the **OlyApiShooting / OlyApiCameraCommon shooting-settings GUI panel** (rodata `"OlyApiShooting::getInterface()->getScene1()/getScene2()"`, `"getSetting MODE_EXPOSE=%d"`, `"MODE_PROGRAM_SHIFT=%d"`, `"isMediaAccess(SD/PP)=%d"`, `"isGpsProcessing=%d"`, `"OlyApiCameraCommon::getInterface()->getEyeFi"`). NULL : *(u32*)(a0 + 792 + idx*4)`. Returns slot `idx` of the panel's 70-entry component table at `+792`. The following routine `0x7e284ab2` is the same iterator body (`0x7e24ff2a` reserve / `0x7e24ff41` step / `0x7e22be56`, then `calls (a1)` on the child's vtable `+4`, compared against `257`=`0x101`) shared by the `0x7e27`/`0x7e29` twins. Enable path = setter `0x7e338f8b` (**`startReserveEventForSync`**, logs `"[GUI App]: startReserveEventForSync"` `0x7e5df460` via `0x7e334de7`); teardown `0x7e339061` clears the latch + resets touchpanel (`get_singleton_60460ea4 0x7e34b8f6`, `touchpanel_clear_drawing_flag 0x7e34ba5a`) then `0x604602ec=0`. Finally call **setDispSync** impl (vtable `+108` → `0x7dff6261`) with the flag, log `"OlyApiCameraCommon::setDispSync() Call arg=%d,result=%d"` (`0x7E5DF41D`), post `gui_post_panel_event 0x7e33581d(5,0,flag)`, return impl result. ## Mode-transition judge (precondition check) trace
Shooting-mode transition **judge** layer: before entering/leaving a mode the firmware evaluates a set
of preconditions and logs each by name with an `OK`/`NG` verdict. The hot emitter takes a condition
**label string** and formats+emits a trace line. Label pool `0x7e42de80` covers the mode judges:
`MODE_MOVIE_JUDGE_NG`, `MODE_MULTI_EXPOSURE_FRAME_JUDGE_NG`/`_BUFFER_SCALE_OFF_JUDGE_OK`,
`MODE_INTERVAL_SHOOTING_JUDGE_NG`, `MODE_MY_MODE_JUDGE_NG`, `MODE_PHOTO_SESSION_JUDGE_NG`,
`MODE_HDR1_HDR2_JUDGE_NG`, `MEDIA_EXIST_JUDGE_OK`/`MEDIA_FORMAT_…`, `isLiveGuide`, `MODE_SCEN…`.
(Cross-refs the RHP interval-shooting / photo-session state machine — see MainSystem/RHP section.)
| address | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7e224095` | `mode_judge_trace` | **198 xrefs** — emits a mode-judge trace line from label string `a0`: builds a 56-byte ctx (`0x7e22352e`), formats (`0x7e223896`), emits via handler `0x7e02fda8` (→ log core `0x7dfc0587`). Sibling variants `0x7e2240b1` (→ `0x7e033d22`), `0x7e2240cd` differ only by emit handler | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e223f45` | 131 | `trace_emit_h5744` | **label-string trace emitter** (handler-`0x7dff5744` variant) — same shape as `mode_judge_trace 0x7e224095` / `ca_op_trace 0x7e224079`: `a0`=label string, stores param `56` into a stack slot, builds trace ctx via `gui_evt_fmt_field 0x7e22352e`, formats via `0x7e223896`, then emits through sink getter `0x7dff5744` (double-checked-lock singleton, lock id 2, cache `0x6044e94c`, 8-byte obj via `operator new 0x7e206a0f`). One of an **address-adjacent bank** of per-sink thunks (`0x7e223f61`→handler `0x7e023dff`, `0x7e223f7d`→`0x7e0244b8`, …) differing only by emit sink. Known callers are SubApp method-entry traces — e.g. module **SubAppMdlBackLight** passes labels `"ctrlLcdBacklight"` (`0x7e42ce93`), `"initSubAppMdlBackLight"`, `"isBackLightOffSubAppMdlBackLight"`, `"cancelBackLightOffSubAppMdlBackLight"`, `"TFTON"`/`"TFTOFF"` (`0x7e42cexx`). **Dead end:** which subsystem log sink `0x6044e94c` feeds (vs the `0x7e02fda8`/`0x7e033d22` sinks of the siblings) not pinned — only the DCL-singleton mechanism is certain | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e42de80` | `mode_judge_string_pool` | `MODE_*_JUDGE_NG`/`_OK` precondition labels (movie, multi-exposure, interval shooting, my-mode, photo session, HDR, media, live guide, scene) | `symbol-map.md` |

## CapPls — playback-info app (app_playinfo_*)
Playback application class **`CapPls`** (module `0x7e086xxx`–`0x7e08bxxx`). Exposes an
`app_playinfo_*` API (names end in `#`, debug/trace tags). Covers: playback **zoom**
(`CapPls::app_playinfo_init_zoom_force`), **panorama play scroll**
(`app_playinfo_panorama_play_scroll_check_func` horizontal/vertical), **image overlay** picture
selection (`app_playinfo_image_overlay_get/set_pic_select_*`), and **Eye-Fi transfer** picture
selection (`app_playinfo_eyefi_transfer_get_pic_select_state` / `get_max_picno_selected`).
String pool at `0x7e3c7439`. DB-open failures log `[CHLS]DB_OPEN_ERR`.
| address | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7e086bbe` | `playinfo_get_pos_sum` | **195 xrefs** — `return *(g_playinfo_base 0x60761570) + *(g_playinfo_off 0x6075e77c)`: combines a base and offset playinfo state word (playback scroll/zoom position). Thin, no args | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e086eb8` | `playinfo_get_geom` | **92 xrefs** — `CapPls_PlayState` playinfo geometry getter. `playinfo_get_geom(a0, out=a1)`: first calls the **output-size** refresh worker **`0x7e08684d`** (`app_playinfo_get_image_output_size`), which derives display **w,h** from playstate accessors `0x7e05c687`/`0x7e05c64a`, stores `w → 0x60761586`, `h → 0x60761588`, and traces `"[<----]app_playinfo_get_image_output_size w/h = %d"` (`0x7e3c76c9`/`0x7e3c76fa`); then copies the **12×u16 (24-byte) playinfo geometry block at `0x60761576`** (w/h live at its +16/+18) into the caller's `out` buffer via a 12-iter `movhu` loop. Block sits just above `g_playinfo_base 0x60761570`. Trace via `trace_log_printf 0x7dfc01bf`. Separate sibling `0x7e086ee1` = `app_playinfo_get_single_option_gain_level` (single gain value, strings `0x7e3c7763`/`"gain_level NULL" 0x7e3c77a0`). 92 call sites read playback display geometry. **Dead end:** exact field layout of the 12-u16 block beyond the +16/+18 output w/h not enumerated | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e08684d` | `playinfo_get_output_size` | `CapPls::app_playinfo_get_image_output_size` — compute display **w,h** via playstate accessors `0x7e05c687`/`0x7e05c64a`, write `w→0x60761586` / `h→0x60761588`, trace both (`0x7e3c76c9`/`0x7e3c76fa`). Refresh step behind `playinfo_get_geom 0x7e086eb8` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e086bce` / `0x7e086bd7` / `0x7e086be0` | `playinfo_get_u16_fd86` / `playinfo_get_154c` / `playinfo_get_u16_156a` | adjacent playinfo state getters: `(u16)*(0x6044fd86)`, `*(0x6076154c)`, `(u16)*(0x6076156a)` | `symbol-map.md` |

## CGuiDrawCtrl — GUI draw controller (live-view "through" display)
GUI draw-controller class (module `0x7e240xxx`). Drives the live-view **through** image
(`reqDrawThrough` → graphics-engine `GESetEvent()`) and black-frame fill (`DrawImageBlack`).
Trace strings `[GUI App]: CGuiDrawCtrl::DrawImageBlack` (`0x7e46603d`),
`CGuiDrawCtrl::reqDrawThrough draw() ret = %d` (`0x7e466065`), `…reqDrawThrough GESetEvent()`
(`0x7e46609d`). Small 12-byte state singleton in the `0x604543xx` GUI bank.
| address | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7e24048a` | `guidrawctrl_instance` | **193 xrefs** — lazy singleton getter: cell `0x604543f8`; if null → `operator new 0x7e206a0f`(12) + ctor `0x7e24047f` + store. Returns the CGuiDrawCtrl state object | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e24047f` | `guidrawctrl_ctor` | ctor for the 12-byte **vtable-less** state struct: `+0`(u16)=1, `+4`=0 | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e24060c` | `guidrawctrl_set_state` | **138 xrefs** — state setter/transition for the draw-controller FSM. Args `a0`=CGuiDrawCtrl instance, `d0`=target state (u16, enum `1..4`; `0` is coerced to `1` unless current is already `1`, which is left unchanged). No-op + return when `*(a0)` already equals target. Otherwise writes target into the state word `*(a0+0)`, then dispatches the transition effect through decoder **`0x7e226eef`** with a per-target `(from, event, idx)` triple (target 2→`(1,3,?)`, 3→`(state,1,2)`, 4→`(2,1,?)`, else→`(1,2,3)`). `0x7e226eef` maps `(from-state, event)`→an effect pair `(d2,d3)`. Typical caller fetches the singleton (`guidrawctrl_instance 0x7e24048a`) then drives it, e.g. `0x7e272b38` sets state `1` after clearing a 26-word array; this is the gate that moves the live-view through-draw pipeline between its phases (`reqDrawThrough`/`DrawImageBlack`). Companion entry points `0x7e240657`/`0x7e240662` push related events (`0x7e226f4a`/`0x7e226f5e`). **Dead end:** semantic name of each numbered state (idle/through/black/…) not pinned — no per-state string | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x604543f8` | `g_guidrawctrl` | CGuiDrawCtrl state singleton cell | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e46603d` | `guidrawctrl_string_pool` | `CGuiDrawCtrl::DrawImageBlack`, `::reqDrawThrough draw() ret=%d`, `::reqDrawThrough GESetEvent()` (methods live in module `0x7e240xxx`, e.g. log sites `0x7e2405ae`/`0x7e2406ca`) | `symbol-map.md` |

## Camera query / settings API facade (CA-app getters)
Module `0x7e20dxxx` is the app-facing **camera state/settings query API**. Each entry logs its
operation name via the trace emitter `0x7e224079` (56-byte ctx, sibling of `mode_judge_trace`),
looks up a manager object and calls its accessor `vtable[1](modeInfo)`, then logs
`result of <op> = SUCCESS/ERROR and modeInfo = …`. Operation-name + result strings in pools
`0x7e42fd94` and `0x7e42ffc0`/`0x7e430007`. Named operations seen: `getSetting`, `getSettingDetail`,
`getSettingUser`, `setSetting`, `getCondition`, `getZoomFrame`, `getThroughZoomRate`,
`getAfAreaType`, `isAfEnable`, `getAvailableShootCount`, `getMovieTime`, `getMediaStateInfo`,
`afState`. (Mirrors the state surface a replacement app must read — see KMP notes.)
| address | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `0x7e20d793` | `ca_getSetting` | **192 xrefs** — `getSetting(d0=modeInfo)`: trace `getSetting`, look up manager (`0x7e224079`), call accessor `vtable[1](modeInfo)`, log `result of getSetting = SUCCESS/ERROR and modeInfo=…`, return value. Hottest query of the facade | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e20d7dc` | `ca_getSettingDetail` | sibling: `getSettingDetail(modeInfo)`, same shape | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e224079` | `ca_op_trace` | per-op trace/lookup helper (56-byte ctx via `0x7e22352e`/`0x7e223896`, handler `0x7e02ec5a`); sibling of `mode_judge_trace 0x7e224095` | `symbol-map.md` |
| `0x7e42fd94` / `0x7e430007` | `ca_api_string_pool` | operation names (`get*`/`set*`/`is*`) + `result of <op> = SUCCESS/ERROR and modeInfo=` templates | `symbol-map.md` |

## Trace/call-logger facade singletons (bank `0x6044e958`+)
A bank of tiny **stateless trace/call-logger facade** objects — each 4 bytes (vtable pointer only,
no state), lazily built by a `get_shootcount_mgr`-shape getter (double-checked lock **slot 2**:
`0x7dfc0587`/`0x7dfc056a`/`0x7dfc057a`, `operator new(4)` `0x7e206a0f`, ctor installs vtable). Each
vtable method logs an operation as `<name>(args)` by resolving a packed **log-context id** (`0x10cxx`
block) through `decode_packed_id 0x7dfc7272` (the `%1s` name token) and formatting via
`trace_log_printf` / `0x7dfc01e6` at level 3, using the shared template pool
`0x7e386100`–`0x7e386260` (`%1s(%d)`, `%1s(%d, %d)`, `%1s(%d, %d, %d)`, enter/exit brace markers
`}{}`/`}h{`/`}_t`); several stamp a `0xdeadbeef` trace sentinel (`0x7dfc498f`). The specific
subsystem each facade serves is **not resolvable statically** — the names live in the RAM id-table
`0x6044e518` that `decode_packed_id` indexes. Same family as `get_shootcount_mgr 0x7e02fda8`.
| address | symbol | role | source |
|---|---|---|---|
